# 51. Inference ledger (mapping waves, docs/32-50 and 52-62)

Every claim marked [inferred] in docs/32-50 (§3) and docs/52-62 (§3b), plus [verified] static claims that a runtime check should back before a fix depends on them, with how to test each one. Rows that group many alike claims say so. Tags as in AGENTS.md.

## 1. How the rows get a verdict

- **Run:** `node tools/harness/queue.ts tools/harness/queues/verify.json` (two jobs, BP and IP, one load each, ~8 min of play each; 0.10.8: repro `verify-v22`, `minCounters` 22, `ThreadedObjects` 0). Each job loads `game-copy/ts3native.asi.verify` with `[Verify] Enabled=1` and every sub-switch on (`Threads`, `Races`, `Costs`, `Memory`), samples the main thread (`mainstack: "main"`) and runs the `verify-sweep` repro (moodlets, bulk outfit change, CAS open/fill/close, thumbnails + save, camera pan). A job refuses before the game starts when the asi is missing, and after the load when `/native` reports counters older than v21 (`minCounters`). The dump after the load also zeroes the `[Verify]` maxima (`/native?vfresetmax=1`), so the run has a live window.
- **Fill:** `node tools/harness/verify-verdicts.ts --run verify-<date>-bp-verify --run verify-<date>-ip-verify [--write]` reads each job's full `/native` dump (`probe-out/queue/<run>-native.json`) and main-thread stacks (`probe-out/profile/<run>-mainstack.jsonl`), applies the rule column over the live window (counts and `*Us` = after - before, maxima since the reset at load; docs/15 [Verify]) and writes `confirmed` or `refuted` with the numbers into empty verdict cells. `review` rows (no rule, or a rule that cannot decide) stay empty for a human.
- **Static only:** the claim cannot be measured at run time (names, versions, roles of uncalled code, licence facts); it stays open until a decompile settles it. **sampler:** existing samplers in a normal profile run (`simstack`, `managed`); repro mode skips them, so these are not part of verify.json. **fix build:** the claim is the expected gain of a candidate, measured only by building it (A/B).

## 2. vf counter names (contract with the [Verify] module)

The rules match `/native` keys by a target and a suffix: `vf<Group><Target><Suffix>`. Target = the function or global address in hex without `0x` (`D5D910`), or a short token (`Device`, `R1`, `Scene`, `SpeedTree`). Thread-role suffix `Main`, `Sim`, `Other` (`Job*` and `Off` count as Other) for call counts by thread; race keys end in `Race` or `Overlap` (two threads inside the guarded pair or overlap group at once; a key that only contains the word, such as `vfRaceC2A840Calls`, is no race key); cost keys end in `Us`, `Calls`, `MaxUs`; memory keys are `vfMem<Target>Bytes`. Counters are cumulative from game start; the helper takes the live window (after - before) when the dump has both. If the module names keys differently, adjust `keysFor` in `verify-verdicts.ts`, not the ledger.

v22 groups (0.10.8): `vfMono` (Mono warm-up entries, arena, GC_free, managed entries), `vfRt` (docs/60 §2 routing sites), `vfEv` (pump passes, remote calls, script message drain, lot-add context); the thread-slot suffixes are the same.

Rules: `main:T` / `sim:T` (only that role hit T), `offmain:T` (Sim + Other > 0), `both:T` (Main and Sim both > 0), `race:T` (any race key > 0), `max:T<N` / `max:T>N` (largest MaxUs below / above N us; the `>` form since 2026-10-16), `seen:T` (any role key > 0; 0 = review), `none:T/D` (T's role keys 0 while D's > 0; T > 0 refutes), `mem:T<N` (below N MB), `stackmain:A[-B]` (main samples in function A or range A-B), `mainshare:A-B>P` (share of main samples in percent), `roles:T` / `cost:T` (print only). Full list in the script header.

## 3b. Ledger, docs/52-62 (added 2026-10-16, probes 0.10.8 / counters v22)

Same columns. `emu` in "how to test" = better settled by the offline emulator harness (docs/63, `tools/emu/`: run the exe's own bytes on chosen inputs with block coverage), the second way to confirm a claim under the PLAN.md rule of 2026-10-16. Repro `verify-v22` = `verify-sweep` plus build/buy enter and exit, 10 objects placed and removed, and a dwell pan (tools/harness/repros.json).

## 4. Corrections after the first run (0.10.7, 2026-10-15)

- I-38-4 and I-39-1 were written `confirmed` from `vfRace*Calls` keys: the race rule matched any key containing `Race`. Both had 0 overlaps (docs/data/results/2026-10-15-verify-findings.md "Not seen"); the cells are empty again and the rule now needs a key ending in `Race` or `Overlap`.
- I-34-6 was written `confirmed` from a negative readout (-10 MB). The readout is dropped (row text); negative memory values are no data now.
- 0xFBA5CC, 0xFD9744 and 0xFE4DC4 are not locks but the static `-1` timeout each module passes to `Lock_Acquire` (`[esp+4]`); the lock is `ecx` [verified asm]. Rows I-36-10, I-42-3, I-47-8 say so; the keys keep those addresses as targets.
- Probes added for 37 rows (31 new probes, 4 slots on existing probes, 4 overlap groups; list in docs/15 [Verify]). Skipped: I-34-5 (no hook entry), I-36-9 (relative call at the entry), I-40-5 (no vf key). I-36-3 moved its marker from 0x779540 (relative call at the entry) to the LoadLayout IC 0x782BC0.

## 5. Size

113 rows in §3: 56 with a rule in verify.json (vf counters or the main-thread sampler; 36 mechanical, 20 `roles`/`cost` for review; 0.10.7 left 3 rows without a probe, §4), 6 repro + observation without a queue step yet, 8 sampler-only (normal profile runs), 6 fix-build gains, 33 static only. Row I-43-1 alone stands for 65 IC rows of docs/43 §3.

§3b (2026-10-16): see §6.

## 6. Additions 2026-10-16 (docs/52-62, probes 0.10.8)

- PLAN.md rule of 2026-10-16: every runtime-checkable claim of docs/52-62 has a row in §3b with a probe, or says why not. 88 rows: 45 live with a rule (32 mechanical: 14 `sim`, 6 `main`, 5 `seen`, 3 `max`, 2 `mainshare`, 1 `race`, 1 `none`; 13 `cost`/`roles` for review; 41 of them on v22 keys, 4 on v20 keys or the main sampler), 12 `emu`, 21 static only, 4 sampler, 3 not built, 2 fix build (S2b, `[RouteAsync]`), 1 covered by I-38-7. Duplicates of earlier rows are not repeated (docs/52 R1 = I-47-5, docs/55 input pump = I-47-3, camera callers = I-47-7).
- Probes (docs/15 `[Verify]` 0.10.8, counters v22, thread slots 46-79): the six Mono warm-up entries of docs/58 §4 (`0xE85020`, `0xEA1E30`, the cctor slow path `0xEA3382` mid-function, `0xE51400`, `0xE78780`, `0xE53070`), the docs/60 §2 sites (`0x628A90`, `0x835240`, `0x835110`, `0x939420`, `0x682760` filtered on the swap's return `0xC2A896`), pump same pass (`0x59A430` + `0x599FC0` + the existing `0x599720` stub), inline and synchronous remote calls (inside the existing `0x7D98C0` stub), script message drain `0x76B1A0`, lot add (`0xABD3C0` with fn `0xAC1130`) with managed entries inside it (`0xE59FD0`, `0xE5A600`, `0xD8A8E0`, `0xD75FA0`), lot scoring `0xC6C290`, glyph `AddPage`/page texture/`ClearPage`, string table rebuild `0x59BDE0`, XInput poll `0x49C5F0`, shader creates per phase (`0x799780`/`0x799810`, startup = before the first `0xC6D970`), load pump `0xECA350`, cursor manager overlap `0x505620`, arena grow `0xD71900`, `GC_free 0xE4BEB0`, and the readout of `[0x11F2D84]`.
- Not hookable at the entry, so tested another way: `Job_Submit 0x59A350` (a `je` in its first 4 bytes) and `RunJobsLocked 0x599E60` (`0x599ED8` jumps to `0x599E65`): the same-pass test uses `ReadyJob` inside a `RunJobs 0x59A430` pass instead.
- Left out on purpose (docs/68): `0x59C5B0` (S3PP LocalizedStringCache), `0xE4D9F0` (S3PP pattern), anything in `0xD81FC3`-`0xD81FE0` (S3SS limiter pattern), no new probe on `0xABFAC0`/`0x7D2DB0`, and `0xAC1130` itself (S3SS detours it; the lot add is timed at its caller `0xABD3C0`). The existing `0xD7F8B0` probe is now behind `ThreadedObjects` (default 0).
- Not built (row says so): UI flush counters (I-52-7), input latency timestamps (I-55-3), `ShaderWatcher_Poll` count (I-53-7), the S2b job-window counters of docs/60 (I-60-11, I-60-12: need `[RouteAsync]`), first-bind time per shader (docs/53 MS1 second half).
- docs/50 I-50-1 ("script `Init` is the expensive per-object step of a lot add") is now doubtful: docs/61 §3 finds no managed handler in a lot add and the 2026-10-16 lotstream queue measured ~4 µs per native add. I-61-5 and I-61-6 test both live.
- Added after the count above (docs/62 §9 full IC rescan): I-62-6 (emu, host `+0x124` is a wake, not a yield) and I-62-7 (needs a managed-entry probe inside three ICs, not built). I-73-4 now has static support.

## 23. Additions 2026-10-18 (docs/90, design SH, slot position helper `0x76F0A0`, gate-12 F-B)

Same columns as §3. The gate-12 family F-B (biggest gate-12 source, `bp-p1b` today: 854 waits, 7.6 s, 12.7 ms/s at `0x62F782`, second frame `0x76F15D`) sits in the slot position helper `0x76F0A0` (not in `IC_Slots_IsSlotEnabledImpl 0x76F000`; the queue job keeps the task's name). SH runs the game's own `0x76F0A0` body under a QL-shared hold so `RgEnter` passes gate 12 and gate-0 hits without the `+0x918` wait. Built offline only; both switches off.

| id | doc § | claim | how to test | repro | expected if true | rule | verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| I-90-2 | 90 §3 | The hold changes no computation of the body (it is a lock, not data), so the held `0x76F0A0` returns the same point and bool as the unheld call whenever the gate-skip answer matches `Contains`/`GetOrInsert` | SlCall runs `g_slOrig` itself; the only divergence is the gate-skip | offline | equal by construction; gate-skip proven in I-90-3 | reasoning | inferred |
| I-90-3 | 90 §4 | SlGate's gate-12 pass (`LtFind` hit/miss) and gate-0 hit decision match the game's `0x628A90 Contains` and `0x62EAB0 GetOrInsert` on random tables | steerro-test section I on the mapped game functions, seeds 1-4 x 3000 | offline | all equal | assert | verified offline (12000 cases, 0 mismatch) |
